/* HELPERS                                                            */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

function normalizeModelId(requestedModel: string): string {
  if (
    !requestedModel ||
    requestedModel === 'gemini-3.8-flash' ||
    requestedModel === 'gemini-flash-latest'
  ) {
    return 'gemini-4-hydrogen';
  }

  return requestedModel;
}

function errorStatus(err: any): number {
  const msg = String(err?.message || '');
  const code = Number(err?.status || err?.code || 0);

  if (code === 429 || /RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|quota|429/i.test(msg)) {
    return 429;
  }

  if (/API key|GEMINI_API_KEY/i.test(msg)) {
    return 401;
  }

  return 500;
}

function sendError(
  res: express.Response,
  err: any,
  fallbackMessage: string
) {
  const status = errorStatus(err);
  let message = err?.message || fallbackMessage;

  if (status === 429) {
    message =
      'Ïðåâûøåíà êâîòà Gemini API. Ïîäîæäèòå èëè âûáåðèòå äðóãóþ ìîäåëü. ' +
      message;
  }

  res.status(status).json({ error: message });
}

type ThinkingHint = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';

interface SamplingConfig {
  temperature?: number;
  topP?: number;
  topK?: number;
  frequencyPenalty?: number;
  presencePenalty?: number;
}

function resolveThinkingConfig(
  modelId: string,
  hint: ThinkingHint
): Record<string, any> | undefined {
  // gemini-3.1-flash-lite ïî óìîë÷àíèþ ðàáîòàåò íà MINIMAL.
  // MEDIUM/HIGH ïîçâîëÿþò ìîäåëè òùàòåëüíåå ïëàíèðîâàòü ñèíòàêñèñ.
  if (modelId === 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite') {
    const level =
      hint === 'high'
        ? ThinkingLevel.HIGH
        : hint === 'medium'
          ? ThinkingLevel.MEDIUM
          : ThinkingLevel.LOW;

    return { thinkingLevel: level };
  }

  if (modelId === 'gemini-4-hydrogen' && hint === 'low') {
    return { thinkingLevel: ThinkingLevel.LOW };
  }

  return undefined;
}

async function callModelOnce(params: {
  model: string;
  contents: string;
  systemInstruction: string;
  thinkingHint?: ThinkingHint;
  responseSchema?: any;
  sampling?: SamplingConfig;
}): Promise<string> {
  const {
    model,
    contents,
    systemInstruction,
    thinkingHint = 'medium',
    responseSchema,
    sampling,
  } = params;

  const thinkingConfig = resolveThinkingConfig(model, thinkingHint);

  const baseConfig: Record<string, any> = {
    systemInstruction,
  };

  if (responseSchema) {
    baseConfig.responseMimeType = 'application/json';
    baseConfig.responseSchema = responseSchema;
  }

  if (thinkingConfig) {
    baseConfig.thinkingConfig = thinkingConfig;
  }

  if (sampling) {
    if (typeof sampling.temperature === 'number') {
      baseConfig.temperature = sampling.temperature;
    }
    if (typeof sampling.topP === 'number') {
      baseConfig.topP = sampling.topP;
    }
    if (typeof sampling.topK === 'number') {
      baseConfig.topK = sampling.topK;
    }
    if (typeof sampling.frequencyPenalty === 'number') {
      baseConfig.frequencyPenalty = sampling.frequencyPenalty;
    }
    if (typeof sampling.presencePenalty === 'number') {
      baseConfig.presencePenalty = sampling.presencePenalty;
    }
  }

  try {
    const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
      model,
      contents,
      config: baseConfig,
    });

    return (response.text || '').trim();
  } catch (err: any) {
    const msg = String(err?.message || '');
    const isUnsupportedConfig =
      !isRateLimitError(err) &&
      ((baseConfig.thinkingConfig && /thinking/i.test(msg)) ||
        /frequencyPenalty|presencePenalty|frequency_penalty|presence_penalty|topK|unknown name|INVALID_ARGUMENT|not supported for this model/i.test(
          msg
        ));

    if (isUnsupportedConfig) {
      const retryConfig = { ...baseConfig };
      if (/thinking/i.test(msg)) {
        delete retryConfig.thinkingConfig;
      }
      delete retryConfig.frequencyPenalty;
      delete retryConfig.presencePenalty;
      delete retryConfig.topK;

      const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
        model,
        contents,
        config: retryConfig,
      });

      return (response.text || '').trim();
    }

    throw err;
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* QUOTA COOLDOWN & HELPER CACHES                                     */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

const modelCooldownUntil = new Map<string, number>();

function isRateLimitError(err: any): boolean {
  const msg = String(err?.message || '');
  const code = Number(err?.status || err?.code || 0);
  return code === 429 || /RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|quota|rate[- ]limit|\b429\b/i.test(msg);
}

function parseQuotaCooldownMs(err: any): number {
  const msg = String(err?.message || '');
  const isDaily = /PerDay|GenerateRequestsPerDay|retry in \d+h/i.test(msg);
  const secMatch = msg.match(/"retryDelay"\s*:\s*"(\d+)s"/i);
  if (secMatch) {
    const ms = parseInt(secMatch[1], 10) * 1000;
    return isDaily ? Math.min(ms, 15 * 60 * 1000) : Math.min(Math.max(ms, 15_000), 90_000);
  }
  return isDaily ? 15 * 60 * 1000 : 60_000;
}

function markModelCooldown(modelId: string, err: any): void {
  if (!isRateLimitError(err)) return;
  const cooldownMs = parseQuotaCooldownMs(err);
  modelCooldownUntil.set(modelId, Date.now() + cooldownMs);
}

function isModelOnCooldown(modelId: string): boolean {
  const until = modelCooldownUntil.get(modelId);
  if (!until) return false;
  if (Date.now() >= until) {
    modelCooldownUntil.delete(modelId);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function buildCandidateModelList(preferredModel: string): string[] {
  const normalized = normalizeModelId(preferredModel);
  const baseOrder = Array.from(
    new Set([normalized, 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite', 'gemini-4-hydrogen'])
  );
  const available = baseOrder.filter((m) => !isModelOnCooldown(m));
  // Åñëè âñå ìîäåëè íà âðåìåííîì êóëäàóíå, âñ¸ ðàâíî ïðîáóåì ðåçåðâíûé ïîðÿäîê (íà÷èíàÿ ñ high-quota Flash Lite)
  return available.length > 0 ? available : ['gemini-3.1-flash-lite', normalized];
}

function hashText(text: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex').slice(0, 24);
}

function setLru<K, V>(map: Map<K, V>, key: K, val: V, maxSize = 40): void {
  if (map.has(key)) map.delete(key);
  map.set(key, val);
  if (map.size > maxSize) {
    const oldest = map.keys().next().value;
    if (oldest !== undefined) map.delete(oldest);
  }
}

const blueprintCache = new Map<string, DonorStyleBlueprint>();
const deSkeletonCache = new Map<string, DeSkeletonResult>();

/**
 * Ãåíåðàöèÿ ñ ïåðåõîäîì íà ðåçåðâíûå ìîäåëè è àâòî-îõëàæäåíèåì èñ÷åðïàííûõ ìîäåëåé.
 */
async function generateWithFallback(
  preferredModel: string,
  contents: string,
  systemInstruction: string,
  thinkingHint: ThinkingHint = 'medium',
  sampling?: SamplingConfig
): Promise<{
  text: string;
  usedModel: string;
  fallbackOccurred: boolean;
}> {
  if (!process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) {
    throw new Error(
      'GEMINI_API_KEY íå çàäàí íà ñåðâåðå (ïðîâåðüòå .env èëè ñåêðåòû ïðîåêòà).'
    );
  }

  const normalized = normalizeModelId(preferredModel);
  const uniqueModels = buildCandidateModelList(normalized);

  let lastError: any = null;

  for (let i = 0; i < uniqueModels.length; i++) {
    const modelToTry = uniqueModels[i];

    try {
      const text = await callModelOnce({
        model: modelToTry,
        contents,
        systemInstruction,
        thinkingHint,
        sampling,
      });

      if (!text) {
        throw new Error(
          `Ìîäåëü ${modelToTry} âåðíóëà ïóñòîé îòâåò (âîçìîæíà áëîêèðîâêà ïî safety-ôèëüòðó).`
        );
      }

      return {
        text,
        usedModel: modelToTry,
        fallbackOccurred: modelToTry !== normalized,
      };
    } catch (err: any) {
      lastError = err;

      if (isRateLimitError(err)) {
        markModelCooldown(modelToTry, err);
        console.log(
          `[Gemini Quota] ${modelToTry} èñ÷åðïàëà ëèìèò  àâòîïåðåêëþ÷åíèå íà ðåçåðâ.`
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          `[Gemini Fallback] ${modelToTry}: ${String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 180)}`
        );
      }

      if (
        /API key not valid|API_KEY_INVALID|GEMINI_API_KEY/i.test(
          String(err?.message || '')
        )
      ) {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  throw lastError || new Error('Íå óäàëîñü ñãåíåðèðîâàòü îòâåò ÷åðåç Gemini API');
}

/**
 * Áåçîïàñíàÿ ãåíåðàöèÿ JSON.
 * Ïðè îøèáêå èëè íåêîððåêòíîì JSON âîçâðàùàåò null.
 */
async function generateJsonSafe<T>(
  preferredModel: string,
  contents: string,
  systemInstruction: string,
  responseSchema: any,
  thinkingHint: ThinkingHint = 'low',
  sampling?: SamplingConfig
): Promise<T | null> {
  if (!process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) {
    return null;
  }

  const models = buildCandidateModelList(preferredModel);

  for (const model of models) {
    try {
      const raw = await callModelOnce({
        model,
        contents,
        systemInstruction,
        thinkingHint,
        responseSchema,
        sampling,
      });

      if (!raw) {
        continue;
      }

      const cleaned = raw
        .replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, '')
        .replace(/\s*```$/i, '')
        .trim();

      return JSON.parse(cleaned) as T;
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (isRateLimitError(err)) {
        markModelCooldown(model, err);
        console.log(`[Gemini JSON Quota] ${model} íà ïàóçå  ïåðåêëþ÷åíèå.`);
      } else {
        console.log(
          `[Gemini JSON Fallback] ${model}: ${String(err?.message || err).slice(0, 160)}`
        );
      }

      if (
        /API key not valid|API_KEY_INVALID|GEMINI_API_KEY/i.test(
          String(err?.message || '')
        )
      ) {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* FACT PRESERVATION                                                  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

/**
 * Ïðèâîäèò ñïèñîê óòðà÷åííûõ ôàêòîâ ê ñîïîñòàâèìîìó âèäó.
 *
 * Èñïîëüçóåòñÿ äëÿ ïðîâåðêè íå òîëüêî êîëè÷åñòâà ïîòåðü,
 * íî è êîíêðåòíûõ ýëåìåíòîâ ñïèñêà.
 */
function getCriticalFactKeys(diag: DiagnosticReport): Set<string> {
  const lostCritical = diag?.factsReport?.lostCritical;

  if (!Array.isArray(lostCritical)) {
    return new Set();
  }

  return new Set(
    lostCritical.map((fact: any) => {
      if (typeof fact === 'string') {
        return fact.trim().toLowerCase();
      }

      try {
        return JSON.stringify(fact);
      } catch {
        return String(fact);
      }
    })
  );
}

/**
 * Ïðîâåðÿåò, ÷òî íîâûé òåêñò íå ïîòåðÿë íè îäíîãî ôàêòà,
 * êîòîðûé óæå ïðèñóòñòâîâàë â ïðåäûäóùåì ðåçóëüòàòå.
 *
 * Íîâûå ïîòåðè çàïðåùåíû äàæå òîãäà, êîãäà îáùåå êîëè÷åñòâî
 * óòðà÷åííûõ ôàêòîâ îñòàëîñü ïðåæíèì.
 */
function factsDoNotRegress(
  prevDiag: DiagnosticReport,
  nextDiag: DiagnosticReport
): boolean {
  const prevFacts = getCriticalFactKeys(prevDiag);
  const nextFacts = getCriticalFactKeys(nextDiag);

  for (const fact of nextFacts) {
    if (!prevFacts.has(fact)) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

/**
 * Åäèíûé êðèòåðèé ïðèíÿòèÿ êàíäèäàòà.
 * Äåòåêòîðíûå ýâðèñòèêè íàìåðåííî íå ó÷àñòâóþò â ðåøåíèè:
 * îíè îñòàþòñÿ òîëüêî äèàãíîñòè÷åñêèìè ïîêàçàòåëÿìè.
 */
function acceptCandidateByDiagnostics(
  prevText: string,
  prevDiag: DiagnosticReport,
  nextText: string,
  nextDiag: DiagnosticReport
): boolean {
  const previous = prevText.trim();
  const next = nextText.trim();

  if (!next || next === previous) return false;
  if (!factsDoNotRegress(prevDiag, nextDiag)) return false;

  const prevLost = prevDiag.factsReport.lostCritical.length;
  const nextLost = nextDiag.factsReport.lostCritical.length;
  if (nextLost > prevLost) return false;

  const prevLeaks = prevDiag.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0;
  const nextLeaks = nextDiag.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0;
  if (nextLeaks > prevLeaks) return false;

  // Çàùèòà îò ñëó÷àéíîãî îáðåçàíèÿ ñóùåñòâåííîé ÷àñòè èñõîäíîãî ðåçóëüòàòà.
  if (next.length < previous.length * 0.72) return false;

  const overallDelta = nextDiag.overallScore - prevDiag.overallScore;
  const dnaDelta = nextDiag.dnaMatchScore - prevDiag.dnaMatchScore;
  const humanDelta = nextDiag.humanScore - prevDiag.humanScore;

  const noMaterialRegression =
    overallDelta >= -1 && dnaDelta >= -2 && humanDelta >= -3;
  const meaningfulImprovement =
    overallDelta >= 0.5 || dnaDelta >= 1 || humanDelta >= 1;

  return noMaterialRegression && meaningfulImprovement;
}

  paragraphs: Array<{
    theme: string;
    propositions: string[];
  }>;

}

}

  finalText: string;
  diagBefore: DiagnosticReport;
  diagAfter: DiagnosticReport;
  detectorAiBefore: number;
  detectorAiAfter: number;
  passes: number;
  usedModel: string;
  fallbackOccurred: boolean;
}

function formatPropositionsInput(
  deSkeleton: DeSkeletonResult,
  rawFallbackText2: string,
  donorProfile: DonorDNAProfile
): string {
  const blocks = deSkeleton.paragraphs
    .map(
      (paragraph, index) =>
        `Áëîê ${index + 1} (${paragraph.theme}):\n${paragraph.propositions
          .map((prop) => `- ${prop}`)
          .join('\n')}`
    )
    .join('\n\n');

  const entities =
    deSkeleton.mandatoryEntities.length > 0
      ? `\nÎÁßÇÀÒÅËÜÍÛÅ ÈÌÅÍÀ, ÄÀÒÛ È ÒÅÐÌÈÍÛ (âêëþ÷èòü 100%): ${deSkeleton.mandatoryEntities.join(', ')}`
      : '';

  return `ÀÒÎÌÀÐÍÛÅ ÒÅÇÈÑÛ È ÔÀÊÒÛ ÄËß ÍÀÏÈÑÀÍÈß (Î×ÈÙÅÍÎ ÎÒ ÈÈ-ÊÀÐÊÀÑÀ ÒÅÊÑÒÀ 2):
${blocks}
${entities}

=========================
ÄËß ÊÎÍÒÐÎËß ÒÎ×ÍÎÑÒÈ (ÈÑÕÎÄÍÛÉ ÒÅÊÑÒ 2  ïðîâåðÿé ôàêòû, íî ÍÅ êîïèðóé åãî ñèíòàêñèñ):
${rawFallbackText2}

=========================

ÇÀÄÀÍÈÅ:
Ñîáåðè èç ýòèõ ôàêòîâ öåëüíûé æèâîé òåêñò â àáñîëþòíîì ãîëîñå, ðèòìå (${donorProfile.avgSentPerPara} ïðåäë./àáç., Burstiness ~${donorProfile.burstiness}) è ñèíòàêñèñå Àâòîðà-Äîíîðà.

Âûâåäè ÒÎËÜÊÎ ãîòîâûé òåêñò áåç çàãîëîâêîâ, êîììåíòàðèåâ è êàâû÷åê.`;
}

function inferGenreFromInput(text: string, hint: string): 'academic' | 'essay' | 'draft' {
  const combined = `${hint}\n${text.slice(0, 1200)}`.toLowerCase();
  if (/äèññåðòàö|ìîíîãðàô|íàó÷í|ìåòîäîëîã|ãèïîòåç|òàáëèö|âûáîðê|ýêñïåðèìåíò|èññëåäîâàí|àêàäåìè÷|ñòàòüÿ/i.test(combined)) {
    return 'academic';
  }
  if (/ýññå|ïóáëèöèñò|î÷åðê|æèâîé|èñòîðè|ôèëîñîô|ðàçìûøëåí|êîëîíêà/i.test(combined)) {
    return 'essay';
  }
  return 'draft';
}

/**
 * Ñêîëüçÿùèé ïîàáçàöíûé ïåðåíîñ äëèííûõ òåêñòîâ (2200+ çíàêîâ, >= 4 ñìûñëîâûõ áëîêà):
 * ïåðåíîñèò áëîêàìè ïî 2 àáçàöà ñ ïåðåäà÷åé ïîñëåäíèõ 2 ïðåäëîæåíèé ïðåäûäóùåãî áëîêà
 * äëÿ ñîõðàíåíèÿ ðèòìè÷åñêîé è ñìûñëîâîé ïðååìñòâåííîñòè áåç «óñòàëîñòè» íà äëèííîì õâîñòå.
 */

 */
async function generateChunkedStyleTransfer(params: {
  deSkeleton: DeSkeletonResult;
  donorProfile: DonorDNAProfile;
  systemInstruction: string;
  model: string;
}): Promise<{ text: string; usedModel: string; fallbackOccurred: boolean }> {
  const { deSkeleton, donorProfile, systemInstruction, model } = params;
  const chunks: Array<DeSkeletonResult['paragraphs']> = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < deSkeleton.paragraphs.length; i += 2) {
    chunks.push(deSkeleton.paragraphs.slice(i, i + 2));
  }

  const generatedParts: string[] = [];
  let usedModel = model;
  let fallbackOccurred = false;

  for (let idx = 0; idx < chunks.length; idx++) {
    const chunkParas = chunks[idx];
    const blocksText = chunkParas
      .map(
        (p, pIdx) =>
          `Áëîê ${idx * 2 + pIdx + 1} (${p.theme}):\n${p.propositions
            .map((prop) => `- ${prop}`)
            .join('\n')}`
      )
      .join('\n\n');

    const bridgeText =
      idx > 0 && generatedParts.length > 0
        ? `\n\nÏÐÅÄÛÄÓÙÈÉ ÔÐÀÃÌÅÍÒ (òîëüêî äëÿ òî÷íîãî ðèòìè÷åñêîãî ñòûêà  ÍÅ ïîâòîðÿé ýòè ôðàçû):\n«${extractLastSentences(generatedParts[generatedParts.length - 1], 2)}»`
        : '';

    const chunkPrompt = `×ÀÑÒÜ ${idx + 1} ÈÇ ${chunks.length} (ÏÎÑËÅÄÎÂÀÒÅËÜÍÛÉ ÏÅÐÅÍÎÑ ÏÎ ÄÎÍÎÐÓ):
${blocksText}

ÊËÞ×ÅÂÛÅ ÈÌÅÍÀ È ÒÅÐÌÈÍÛ ÒÅÊÑÒÀ 2 (ñîõðàíèòü òå, ÷òî îòíîñÿòñÿ ê ýòèì áëîêàì): ${deSkeleton.mandatoryEntities.join(', ') || 'ïî ñìûñëó áëîêîâ'}${bridgeText}

ÎÐÈÅÍÒÈÐ ÐÈÒÌÀ ÄÎÍÎÐÀ: ${donorProfile.avgSentPerPara} ïðåäë./àáç., ñðåäíÿÿ äëèíà ôðàçû ${donorProfile.avgLen} ñëîâ, òèï ôèíàëà àáçàöà: ${donorProfile.paragraphClosingStyle}.
Íàïèøè ÒÎËÜÊÎ ýòó ÷àñòü (${chunkParas.length} àáç.) ÷èñòûì òåêñòîì áåç çàãîëîâêîâ è êîììåíòàðèåâ.`;

    const res = await generateWithFallback(
      usedModel,
      chunkPrompt,
      systemInstruction,
      idx === 0 ? 'high' : 'medium',
      { temperature: 0.96, topP: 0.95, frequencyPenalty: 0.3, presencePenalty: 0.2 }
    );

    usedModel = res.usedModel;
    fallbackOccurred = fallbackOccurred || res.fallbackOccurred;
    generatedParts.push(deterministicPolish(res.text, donorProfile));
  }

  return {
    text: deterministicPolish(generatedParts.join('\n\n'), donorProfile),
    usedModel,
    fallbackOccurred,
  };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* GENERIC CONVERGENCE LOOP                                           */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

/**
 * Óíèâåðñàëüíûé ìíîãîïðîõîäíûé öèêë äëÿ âêëàäîê:
 * Humanizer / Draft / Academic.
 *
 * Ìîæíî ïåðåäàòü óæå ðàññ÷èòàííóþ äèàãíîñòèêó, ÷òîáû íå âûïîëíÿòü
 * ïîâòîðíûé àíàëèç òîãî æå ñàìîãî òåêñòà.
 */
async function runGenericConvergenceLoop(params: {
  initialText: string;
  sourceText: string;
  model: string;
  maxCycles: number;
  initialDiagnostic?: DiagnosticReport;
  shields?: DetectorShields;
}): Promise<GenericConvergenceResult> {
  const {
    initialText,
    sourceText,
    model,
    maxCycles,
    initialDiagnostic,
    shields,
  } = params;

  let currentText = deterministicPolish(initialText);

  let currentDiag =
    initialDiagnostic ||
    diagnoseTextDefects(currentText, undefined, sourceText);

  let currentDetector = evaluateDetectorFeedback(currentText, shields);

  const diagBefore = currentDiag;
  const detectorAiBefore = currentDetector.compositeAiPct;

  let currentModel = model;
  let fallbackOccurred = false;

  let passes = 1;
  let stalled = 0;

  for (let cycle = 1; cycle <= maxCycles; cycle++) {
    // Åñëè êà÷åñòâî òåêñòà óæå äîñòàòî÷íî âûñîêîå, äîïîëíèòåëüíûå çàïðîñû íå íóæíû.
    if (
      !currentDiag.needsRefinement &&
      currentDiag.overallScore >= 94
    ) {
      break;
    }

    // Äâå íåóäà÷íûå ïîïûòêè ïîäðÿä îçíà÷àþò, ÷òî äàëüíåéøèå çàïðîñû
    // ñ âûñîêîé âåðîÿòíîñòüþ íå äàäóò ïîëåçíîãî óëó÷øåíèÿ.
    if (stalled >= 2) {
      break;
    }

    try {
      const hasGlobalIssue = currentDiag.issues.some(
        (issue) =>
          issue.type === 'burstiness' ||
          issue.type === 'donorLengthDrift' ||
          issue.type === 'factLoss' ||
          issue.type === 'ttr' ||
          issue.type === 'donorMarkers' ||
          issue.type === 'nominalizationOverload' ||
          issue.type === 'negationShift'
      );

      const combinedLocalIds = Array.from(
        new Set(currentDiag.issues.flatMap((i) => i.sentenceIndexes))
      ).filter((id) => id > 0);

      const hasOnlyLocal =
        combinedLocalIds.length > 0 &&
        !hasGlobalIssue &&
        currentDiag.issues.every((issue) => issue.sentenceIndexes.length > 0);

      let candidateRaw = '';

      // Õèðóðãè÷åñêàÿ ïðàâêà èñïîëüçóåòñÿ òîëüêî ïðè ëîêàëüíûõ äåôåêòàõ äèàãíîñòèêè.
      if (hasOnlyLocal && stalled === 0) {
        const patchPrompt = buildSurgicalPatchPrompt(
          currentText,
          currentDiag,
          undefined,
          null,
          []
        );

        const patchRes = await generateJsonSafe<{
          patches: Array<{
            sentenceId: number;
            rewritten: string;
          }>;
        }>(
          'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
          patchPrompt.contents,
          patchPrompt.systemInstruction,
          SURGICAL_PATCH_RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
          'medium',
          { temperature: 0.4, topP: 0.9 }
        );

        if (patchRes?.patches?.length) {
          candidateRaw = applySentencePatches(
            currentText,
            patchRes.patches
          );
        }
      }

      // Åñëè ëîêàëüíàÿ ïðàâêà íå äàëà ðåçóëüòàòà èëè ïðîáëåìà ãëîáàëüíàÿ,
      // èñïîëüçóåì îáùèé refinement ïî ñîäåðæàòåëüíîé äèàãíîñòèêå.
      if (!candidateRaw) {
        const refine = buildRefinementPrompt(
          currentText,
          currentDiag,
          undefined,
          null,
          [],
          sourceText
        );

        const passRes = await generateWithFallback(
          currentModel,
          refine.contents,
          refine.systemInstruction,
          'high',
          { temperature: 0.86, topP: 0.94, frequencyPenalty: 0.25 }
        );

        candidateRaw = passRes.text;

        currentModel = passRes.usedModel;
        fallbackOccurred =
          fallbackOccurred || passRes.fallbackOccurred;
      }

      const candidate = deterministicPolish(candidateRaw);

      const candidateDiag = diagnoseTextDefects(
        candidate,
        undefined,
        sourceText
      );
      const candidateDetector = evaluateDetectorFeedback(candidate, shields);

      if (
        acceptCandidateByDiagnostics(
          currentText,
          currentDiag,
          candidate,
          candidateDiag
        )
      ) {
        currentText = candidate;
        currentDiag = candidateDiag;
        currentDetector = candidateDetector;

        passes++;
        stalled = 0;
      } else {
        stalled++;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      console.log(
        `[Convergence Loop cycle ${cycle}] stopped cleanly: ${msg.slice(0, 180)}`
      );

      break;
    }
  }

  return {
    finalText: currentText,
    diagBefore,
    diagAfter: currentDiag,
    detectorAiBefore,
    detectorAiAfter: currentDetector.compositeAiPct,
    passes,
    usedModel: currentModel,
    fallbackOccurred,
  };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* 1. STYLE DNA TRANSFER                                              */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

app.post('/api/style-dna-transfer', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      text1,
      text2,
      flags = {},
      shields = {},
      enable20CycleCheck,
      model = 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
    } = req.body;

    if (
      typeof text1 !== 'string' ||
      typeof text2 !== 'string' ||
      !text1.trim() ||
      !text2.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Òåêñò 1 è Òåêñò 2 îáÿçàòåëüíû',
      });
    }

    const runDeepConvergence: boolean =
      enable20CycleCheck ?? !!flags.twentyCycles;

    const normalizedModel = normalizeModelId(model);

    const donorProfile = extractDonorDNAProfile(text1);
    const donorDetectorBaseline = computeDonorDetectorBaseline(text1, shields);

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    /* ÝÒÀÏ 1. ×ÅÐÒ¨Æ ÄÎÍÎÐÀ È ÄÅ-ÑÊÅËÅÒÈÇÀÖÈß (Ñ ÊÝØÅÌ + FLASH LITE)*/
    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */

    let styleBlueprint: DonorStyleBlueprint | null = null;
    let deSkeleton: DeSkeletonResult | null = null;

    if (runDeepConvergence) {
      const t1Hash = hashText(text1.trim());
      const t2Hash = hashText(text2.trim());

      const cachedBp = blueprintCache.get(t1Hash) || null;
      const cachedDs = deSkeletonCache.get(t2Hash) || null;

      // Âñïîìîãàòåëüíûå JSON-ýòàïû âñåãäà ïóñêàåì ÷åðåç âûñîêîêâîòíóþ Flash Lite,
      // ÷òîáû íå ñæèãàòü äíåâíîé ëèìèò gemini-4-hydrogen (20 req/day íà Free Tier).
      const helperModel = 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite';

      const [bpRes, dsRes] = await Promise.all([
        cachedBp
          ? Promise.resolve(cachedBp)
          : (() => {
              const bpPrompt = buildDonorBlueprintPrompt(text1);
              return generateJsonSafe<DonorStyleBlueprint>(
                helperModel,
                bpPrompt.contents,
                bpPrompt.systemInstruction,
                BLUEPRINT_RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
                'medium',
                { temperature: 0.35, topP: 0.9 }
              );
            })(),

        cachedDs
          ? Promise.resolve(cachedDs)
          : text2.trim().length >= 180
          ? (() => {
              const dsPrompt = buildDeSkeletonPrompt(text2);
              return generateJsonSafe<DeSkeletonResult>(
                helperModel,
                dsPrompt.contents,
                dsPrompt.systemInstruction,
                PROPOSITIONS_RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
                'medium',
                { temperature: 0.3, topP: 0.9 }
              );
            })()
          : Promise.resolve(null),
      ]);

      if (bpRes) {
        styleBlueprint = bpRes;
        if (!cachedBp) setLru(blueprintCache, t1Hash, bpRes);
      }

      if (dsRes && dsRes.paragraphs?.length > 0) {
        deSkeleton = dsRes;
        if (!cachedDs) setLru(deSkeletonCache, t2Hash, dsRes);
      }
    }

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    /* ÑÈÑÒÅÌÍÛÉ ÐÅÃËÀÌÅÍÒ È ÀÍÒÈ-ÓÒÅ×ÊÀ ÄÎÍÎÐÀ                       */
    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */

    const forbiddenDonorTerms = extractDonorForbiddenTerms(text1, text2);

    const promptSections = buildEnginePromptSections({
      flags,
      shields,
      enable20CycleCheck: runDeepConvergence,
      donorProfile,
      styleBlueprint,
      forbiddenDonorTerms,
      mode: 'style-dna',
    });

    const systemInstruction = promptSections.join('\n\n');

    const directContents = `ÒÅÊÑÒ 1 (ÎÁÐÀÇÅÖ ÑÒÈËß  ÄÎÍÎÐ, ÊÎÏÈÐÓÉ ÒÎËÜÊÎ ÑÈÍÒÀÊÑÈÑ È ÃÎËÎÑ):
${text1}

=========================

ÒÅÊÑÒ 2 (ÌÀÒÅÐÈÀË ÄËß ÏÅÐÅÐÀÁÎÒÊÈ  ÑÎÕÐÀÍÈ 100% ÔÀÊÒÎÂ):
${text2}

=========================

Âûâåäè ÒÎËÜÊÎ ãîòîâûé ðåçóëüòèðóþùèé òåêñò áåç âñòóïèòåëüíûõ ðåïëèê, áåç êàâû÷åê è áåç ïîñëåñëîâèé.`;

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    /* ÝÒÀÏ 2. ÃÅÍÅÐÀÖÈß ÊÀÍÄÈÄÀÒÎÂ (MULTI-TEMP SAMPLING)             */
    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */

    let bestText = '';
    let bestDiag: DiagnosticReport | null = null;
    let bestDet: DetectorFeedbackReport | null = null;
    let chunkedTransfer = false;

    let usedModel = normalizedModel;
    let fallbackOccurred = false;

    const preferHighQuotaDual =
      !isModelOnCooldown('gemini-3.1-flash-lite') &&
      (normalizedModel === 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite' || isModelOnCooldown(normalizedModel));

    const shouldUseChunkedCandidate = Boolean(
      runDeepConvergence &&
        deSkeleton &&
        deSkeleton.paragraphs.length >= 4 &&
        text2.trim().length >= 2200
    );

    if (runDeepConvergence && deSkeleton && preferHighQuotaDual) {
      const propContents = formatPropositionsInput(
        deSkeleton,
        text2,
        donorProfile
      );

      const candidatePromises: Array<
        Promise<{
          text: string;
          usedModel: string;
          fallbackOccurred: boolean;
          isChunked?: boolean;
        }>
      > = [
        // Êàíäèäàò A: äå-ñêåëåò + ñáàëàíñèðîâàííàÿ æèâîñòü (T=0.92)
        generateWithFallback(
          normalizedModel,
          propContents,
          systemInstruction,
          'high',
          { temperature: 0.92, topP: 0.95, frequencyPenalty: 0.25, presencePenalty: 0.15 }
        ),
        // Êàíäèäàò B: ïðÿìîé ïåðåíîñ + âûñîêàÿ ñèíòàêñè÷åñêàÿ âàðèàòèâíîñòü (T=1.12)
        generateWithFallback(
          normalizedModel,
          directContents,
          systemInstruction,
          'medium',
          { temperature: 1.12, topP: 0.96, frequencyPenalty: 0.35, presencePenalty: 0.25 }
        ),
        // Êàíäèäàò C: íà äëèííûõ òåêñòàõ  ñêîëüçÿùèé ïîàáçàöíûé ïåðåíîñ, èíà÷å high-temp (T=1.24)
        shouldUseChunkedCandidate
          ? generateChunkedStyleTransfer({
              deSkeleton,
              donorProfile,
              systemInstruction,
              model: 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
            }).then((r) => ({ ...r, isChunked: true }))
          : generateWithFallback(
              'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
              propContents,
              systemInstruction,
              'low',
              { temperature: 1.24, topP: 0.97, frequencyPenalty: 0.4, presencePenalty: 0.3 }
            ),
      ];

      const settledResults = await Promise.allSettled(candidatePromises);

      const candidates: Array<{
        text: string;
        diag: DiagnosticReport;
        det: DetectorFeedbackReport;
        model: string;
        fallback: boolean;
        isChunked: boolean;
      }> = [];

      for (const result of settledResults) {
        if (result.status !== 'fulfilled' || !result.value.text) {
          continue;
        }

        const polished = deterministicPolish(
          result.value.text,
          donorProfile
        );

        const diag = diagnoseTextDefects(
          polished,
          donorProfile,
          text2
        );
        const det = evaluateDetectorFeedback(
          polished,
          shields,
          donorDetectorBaseline
        );

        candidates.push({
          text: polished,
          diag,
          det,
          model: result.value.usedModel,
          fallback: result.value.fallbackOccurred,
          isChunked: Boolean(result.value.isChunked),
        });
      }

      if (candidates.length === 0) {
        const firstRejected = settledResults.find(
          (r): r is PromiseRejectedResult => r.status === 'rejected'
        );
        throw (
          firstRejected?.reason ||
          new Error('Âñå ïàðàëëåëüíûå ïîïûòêè ãåíåðàöèè çàâåðøèëèñü áåç ðåçóëüòàòà.')
        );
      }

      // Ñíà÷àëà ñðàâíèâàåì ñîõðàííîñòü ôàêòîâ è îòñóòñòâèå óòå÷êè òåìû äîíîðà,
      // çàòåì êà÷åñòâî ïåðåíîñà ïî ñîäåðæàòåëüíîé äèàãíîñòèêå è ñîâïàäåíèþ Style DNA.
      candidates.sort((a, b) => {
        const lostDiff =
          a.diag.factsReport.lostCritical.length -
          b.diag.factsReport.lostCritical.length;

        if (lostDiff !== 0) {
          return lostDiff;
        }

        const leakDiff =
          (a.diag.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0) -
          (b.diag.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0);

        if (leakDiff !== 0) {
          return leakDiff;
        }

        if (a.diag.overallScore !== b.diag.overallScore) {
          return b.diag.overallScore - a.diag.overallScore;
        }

        if (a.diag.dnaMatchScore !== b.diag.dnaMatchScore) {
          return b.diag.dnaMatchScore - a.diag.dnaMatchScore;
        }

        return b.diag.humanScore - a.diag.humanScore;
      });

      bestText = candidates[0].text;
      bestDiag = candidates[0].diag;
      bestDet = candidates[0].det;
      usedModel = candidates[0].model;
      fallbackOccurred = candidates[0].fallback;
      chunkedTransfer = candidates[0].isChunked;
    } else {
      // Äëÿ ìàëîëèìèòíîé Hydrogen (20 req/day) äåëàåì 1 òî÷íûé ñèíòåç, îïèðàÿñü íà äå-ñêåëåò/÷åðò¸æ îò Flash Lite
      const primaryInput =
        deSkeleton
          ? formatPropositionsInput(deSkeleton, text2, donorProfile)
          : directContents;

      const pass1 = await generateWithFallback(
        normalizedModel,
        primaryInput,
        systemInstruction,
        'high',
        { temperature: 0.98, topP: 0.95, frequencyPenalty: 0.25, presencePenalty: 0.15 }
      );

      bestText = deterministicPolish(
        pass1.text,
        donorProfile
      );

      bestDiag = diagnoseTextDefects(
        bestText,
        donorProfile,
        text2
      );
      bestDet = evaluateDetectorFeedback(
        bestText,
        shields,
        donorDetectorBaseline
      );

      usedModel = pass1.usedModel;
      fallbackOccurred =
        pass1.fallbackOccurred || pass1.usedModel !== normalizedModel;
    }

    if (!bestDiag || !bestDet) {
      throw new Error('Íå óäàëîñü ñôîðìèðîâàòü äèàãíîñòè÷åñêèé îò÷¸ò.');
    }

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    /* ÝÒÀÏ 3. ÏÐÎÂÅÐÊÀ ÏÅÐÅÍÎÑÀ È ÑÕÎÄÈÌÎÑÒÜ ÏÎ STYLE DNA             */
    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */

    const diagBefore = bestDiag;
    const detectorBefore = bestDet;

    const factsLostBefore =
      diagBefore.factsReport.lostCritical.length;

    let passes = 1;
    let cyclesRun = 0;

    let criticScore: number | null = null;
    let criticRuns = 0;
    let lastCriticPass = 0;

    let pendingCriticDirectives: string[] = [];
    let pendingCriticIds: number[] = [];

    let stopReason = '100% äîïóñê ñ ïåðâîãî ñèíòåçà';

    const MAX_CONVERGENCE_CYCLES = 6;

    let stalledCycles = 0;



    if (runDeepConvergence) {
      for (
        let cycle = 1;
        cycle <= MAX_CONVERGENCE_CYCLES;
        cycle++
      ) {
        /* ---------------------------------------------------------- */
        /* ÍÅÇÀÂÈÑÈÌÛÉ LLM-ÊÐÈÒÈÊ (äî 2 ïðîõîäîâ: ñòàðò >= 76 è ôèíàë) */
        /* ---------------------------------------------------------- */

        const canInvokeCritic =
          bestDiag.factsReport.lostCritical.length === 0 &&
          (bestDiag.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0) === 0 &&
          bestDiag.overallScore >= 76 &&
          criticRuns < 2 &&
          (criticRuns === 0 || passes > lastCriticPass);

        if (canInvokeCritic) {
          criticRuns++;
          lastCriticPass = passes;

          try {
            const criticPrompt = buildStyleCriticPrompt(
              text1,
              text2,
              bestText,
              donorProfile,
              styleBlueprint
            );

            const criticRes =
              await generateJsonSafe<CriticResult>(
                'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
                criticPrompt.contents,
                criticPrompt.systemInstruction,
                CRITIC_RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
                'medium',
                { temperature: 0.25, topP: 0.9 }
              );

            if (!criticRes) {
              stopReason =
                'Ïðîéäåíû âñå àëãîðèòìè÷åñêèå ïðîâåðêè êà÷åñòâà';
              break;
            }

            const scores = [
              criticRes.styleFidelityScore,
              criticRes.humanNaturalnessScore,
              criticRes.factCompletenessScore,
            ];

            // Íåêîððåêòíûå îöåíêè íå äîëæíû âëèÿòü íà ðåøåíèå.
            const validScores = scores.every(
              (score) =>
                typeof score === 'number' &&
                Number.isFinite(score) &&
                score >= 0 &&
                score <= 100
            );

            if (!validScores) {
              stopReason =
                'Êðèòèê âåðíóë íåêîððåêòíûå îöåíêè; ñîõðàí¸í ëó÷øèé ðåçóëüòàò';
              break;
            }

            criticScore = Math.round(
              scores.reduce((sum, score) => sum + score, 0) /
                scores.length
            );

            if (
              criticRes.passed &&
              criticRes.styleFidelityScore >= 88 &&
              criticRes.factCompletenessScore >= 90
            ) {
              stopReason =
                `Ïðîéäåíû ïðîâåðêè ñîõðàííîñòè ôàêòîâ è Style DNA; îöåíêà êðèòèêà ${criticScore}/100`;
              break;
            }

            pendingCriticDirectives = Array.isArray(
              criticRes.actionableDirectives
            )
              ? criticRes.actionableDirectives
                  .filter(
                    (directive) =>
                      typeof directive === 'string' &&
                      directive.trim().length > 0
                  )
                  .slice(0, 5)
              : [];

            pendingCriticIds = Array.isArray(
              criticRes.suspiciousSentenceIds
            )
              ? criticRes.suspiciousSentenceIds
                  .filter(
                    (id) =>
                      Number.isInteger(id) &&
                      id >= 0
                  )
                  .slice(0, 6)
              : [];

            // Åñëè êðèòèê íå ïðîø¸ë ïðîâåðêó, íî íå óêàçàë,
            // ÷òî èìåííî íóæíî èñïðàâèòü, íîâûé çàïðîñ áåññìûñëåíåí.
            if (
              !criticRes.passed &&
              pendingCriticDirectives.length === 0 &&
              pendingCriticIds.length === 0
            ) {
              stopReason =
                'Êðèòèê íå ïðåäîñòàâèë êîíêðåòíûõ çàìå÷àíèé; ñîõðàí¸í ëó÷øèé ðåçóëüòàò';
              break;
            }
          } catch (criticError) {
            const msg = criticError instanceof Error ? criticError.message : String(criticError);
            console.log(
              `[Style Critic] skipped: ${msg.slice(0, 180)}`
            );

            stopReason =
              'Ïðîéäåíû àëãîðèòìè÷åñêèå ïðîâåðêè; êðèòèê íåäîñòóïåí';
            break;
          }
        } else if (
          !bestDiag.needsRefinement &&
          bestDiag.overallScore >= 94 &&
          criticRuns >= 1
        ) {
          stopReason =
            `Äîñòèãíóò öåëåâîé óðîâåíü êà÷åñòâà (${bestDiag.overallScore}/100)`;
          break;
        }

        if (stalledCycles >= 2) {
          stopReason =
            'Äîñòèãíóò óñòîé÷èâûé ìàêñèìóì (äàëüíåéøèå ïðàâêè íå óëó÷øàþò ìåòðèêó)';
          break;
        }

        cyclesRun++;

        try {
          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */
          /* ÎÏÐÅÄÅËÅÍÈÅ ÃËÎÁÀËÜÍÛÕ ÄÅÔÅÊÒÎÂ                          */
          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */

          const hasGlobalIssue = bestDiag.issues.some(
            (issue) =>
              issue.type === 'burstiness' ||
              issue.type === 'donorLengthDrift' ||
              issue.type === 'factLoss' ||
              issue.type === 'ttr' ||
              issue.type === 'donorMarkers' ||
              issue.type === 'nominalizationOverload' ||
              issue.type === 'negationShift'
          );

          const mergedCriticDirectives = pendingCriticDirectives.slice(0, 5);

          const mergedLocalIds = Array.from(
            new Set([
              ...pendingCriticIds,
              ...bestDiag.issues.flatMap((issue) => issue.sentenceIndexes),
            ])
          )
            .filter((id) => Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0)
            .slice(0, 6);

          const hasLocalIds =
            bestDiag.issues.some(
              (issue) => issue.sentenceIndexes.length > 0
            ) ||
            mergedLocalIds.length > 0;

          let candidateRaw = '';

          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */
          /* ÕÈÐÓÐÃÈ×ÅÑÊÀß ÏÐÀÂÊÀ                                     */
          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */

          if (
            !hasGlobalIssue &&
            hasLocalIds &&
            stalledCycles === 0
          ) {
            const syntheticDiag: DiagnosticReport =
              mergedLocalIds.length > 0 &&
              bestDiag.issues.length === 0
                ? {
                    ...bestDiag,
                    issues: [
                      {
                        type: 'cliche',
                        severity: 'major',
                        sentenceIndexes: mergedLocalIds,
                        message:
                          `Ïðåäëîæåíèÿ ¹${mergedLocalIds.join(', ¹')} ` +
                          'òðåáóþò ïðîâåðêè ïî ñîäåðæàòåëüíîé äèàãíîñòèêå è ñîîòâåòñòâèþ ñòèëþ äîíîðà.',
                      },
                    ],
                  }
                : bestDiag;

            const patchPrompt = buildSurgicalPatchPrompt(
              bestText,
              syntheticDiag,
              donorProfile,
              styleBlueprint,
              mergedCriticDirectives
            );

            const patchRes = await generateJsonSafe<{
              patches: Array<{
                sentenceId: number;
                rewritten: string;
              }>;
            }>(
              'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
              patchPrompt.contents,
              patchPrompt.systemInstruction,
              SURGICAL_PATCH_RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
              'medium',
              { temperature: 0.42, topP: 0.9 }
            );

            if (patchRes?.patches?.length) {
              candidateRaw = applySentencePatches(
                bestText,
                patchRes.patches
              );
            }
          }

          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */
          /* ÎÁÙÈÉ REFINEMENT                                         */
          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */

          if (!candidateRaw) {
            const refinePrompt = buildRefinementPrompt(
              bestText,
              bestDiag,
              donorProfile,
              styleBlueprint,
              mergedCriticDirectives,
              text2
            );

            const passN = await generateWithFallback(
              usedModel,
              refinePrompt.contents,
              refinePrompt.systemInstruction,
              'high',
              { temperature: 0.85, topP: 0.94, frequencyPenalty: 0.25 }
            );

            candidateRaw = passN.text;

            // Îáíîâëÿåì èíôîðìàöèþ î ìîäåëè, åñëè ñðàáîòàë fallback.
            usedModel = passN.usedModel;

            fallbackOccurred =
              fallbackOccurred || passN.fallbackOccurred;
          }

          const candidate = deterministicPolish(
            candidateRaw,
            donorProfile
          );

          const candidateDiag = diagnoseTextDefects(
            candidate,
            donorProfile,
            text2
          );
          const candidateDet = evaluateDetectorFeedback(
            candidate,
            shields,
            donorDetectorBaseline
          );

          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */
          /* ÏÐÎÂÅÐÊÀ ÊÀÍÄÈÄÀÒÀ ÏÎ ÔÀÊÒÀÌ È ÊÀ×ÅÑÒÂÓ STYLE DNA       */
          /* -------------------------------------------------------- */

          if (
            acceptCandidateByDiagnostics(
              bestText,
              bestDiag,
              candidate,
              candidateDiag
            )
          ) {
            bestText = candidate;
            bestDiag = candidateDiag;
            bestDet = candidateDet;

            passes++;
            stalledCycles = 0;

            pendingCriticDirectives = [];
            pendingCriticIds = [];
          } else {
            stalledCycles++;
          }
        } catch (cycleError) {
          const msg = cycleError instanceof Error ? cycleError.message : String(cycleError);
          console.log(
            `[Convergence Cycle ${cycle}] gracefully stopped: ${msg.slice(0, 180)}`
          );

          stopReason =
            'Çàâåðøåíî äîñðî÷íî (îãðàíè÷åíèå API íà ïîçäíåì öèêëå), ñîõðàí¸í ëó÷øèé ðåçóëüòàò';

          break;
        }
      }

      if (cyclesRun >= MAX_CONVERGENCE_CYCLES) {
        stopReason =
          `Âûïîëíåíî ${cyclesRun} öèêëîâ äîæèìà ` +
          `(èòîãîâûé èíäåêñ ${bestDiag.overallScore}/100)`;
      }
    }

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */
    /* ÈÒÎÃÎÂÛÉ ÎÒ×¨Ò                                                 */
    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- */

    const diagAfter = bestDiag;

    res.json({
      result: bestText,
      usedModel,
      fallbackOccurred,

      refinementMeta: {
        passes,
        cyclesRun,

        issuesDetected: diagBefore.issues.length,
        issuesRemaining: diagAfter.issues.length,

        burstinessBefore: diagBefore.burstiness,
        burstinessAfter: diagAfter.burstiness,

        clichesBefore: diagBefore.foundCliches.length,
        clichesAfter: diagAfter.foundCliches.length,

        donorBurstinessTarget: donorProfile.burstiness,

        factsLostBefore,
        factsLostAfter: diagAfter.factsReport.lostCritical.length,
        factsPreservedPct: diagAfter.factsReport.preservedPct,

        dnaMatchBefore: diagBefore.dnaMatchScore,
        dnaMatchAfter: diagAfter.dnaMatchScore,

        humanScoreBefore: diagBefore.humanScore,
        humanScoreAfter: diagAfter.humanScore,

        overallScoreAfter: diagAfter.overallScore,

        detectorAiBefore: detectorBefore.compositeAiPct,
        detectorAiAfter: bestDet.compositeAiPct,
        detectorDistanceAfter: bestDet.donorDistance,
        donorLeakCount: diagAfter.factsReport.donorLeakTerms?.length ?? 0,

        blueprintExtracted: !!styleBlueprint,
        deSkeletonized: !!deSkeleton,
        chunkedTransfer,
        criticRuns,

        criticScore,
        stopReason,
      },
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Style DNA error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà îáðàáîòêè');
  }
});

/* 2. TRANSFORM TEXT                                                  */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

app.post('/api/transform-text', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      text,
      promptStrategy = '',
      styleIntensity = 9,
      flags = {},
      shields = {},
      model = 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',





    } = req.body;

    if (typeof text !== 'string' || !text.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Òåêñò îáÿçàòåëåí',
      });
    }

    const runRefine = !!flags.twentyCycles;

    const normalizedModel = normalizeModelId(model);
    const inferredGenre = inferGenreFromInput(text, promptStrategy);
    const archetypeProfile = getGenreArchetypeProfile(inferredGenre);

    const promptSections = buildEnginePromptSections({
      flags,
      shields,
      enable20CycleCheck: runRefine,
      donorProfile: archetypeProfile,
      styleIntensity,
      promptStrategy,
      mode: 'humanizer',
    });

    const systemInstruction = promptSections.join('\n\n');

    const contents =
      `ÈÑÕÎÄÍÛÉ ÒÅÊÑÒ ÄËß ÄÅ-ÈÑÊÓÑÑÒÂÅÍÍÎÑÒÈ:\n${text}\n\n` +
      'Âûâåäè ÒÎËÜÊÎ ïåðåðàáîòàííûé æèâîé àâòîðñêèé òåêñò áåç âñòóïëåíèé è êîììåíòàðèåâ.';

    const pass1 = await generateWithFallback(
      normalizedModel,
      contents,
      systemInstruction,
      'high',
      { temperature: 1.0, topP: 0.95, frequencyPenalty: 0.28, presencePenalty: 0.15 }
    );

    let finalText = deterministicPolish(pass1.text);

    let diagBefore = diagnoseTextDefects(
      finalText,
      undefined,
      text
    );

    let diagAfter = diagBefore;
    let detectorAiBefore = evaluateDetectorFeedback(finalText, shields).compositeAiPct;
    let detectorAiAfter = detectorAiBefore;

    let passes = 1;

    let usedModel = pass1.usedModel;
    let fallbackOccurred = pass1.fallbackOccurred;

    if (runRefine && styleIntensity >= 5) {
      const loopRes = await runGenericConvergenceLoop({
        initialText: finalText,
        sourceText: text,
        model: usedModel,
        maxCycles: 4,
        initialDiagnostic: diagBefore,
        shields,
      });

      finalText = loopRes.finalText;

      diagBefore = loopRes.diagBefore;
      diagAfter = loopRes.diagAfter;
      detectorAiBefore = loopRes.detectorAiBefore;
      detectorAiAfter = loopRes.detectorAiAfter;

      passes = loopRes.passes;

      usedModel = loopRes.usedModel;

      fallbackOccurred =
        fallbackOccurred || loopRes.fallbackOccurred;
    }

    res.json({
      transformedText: finalText,
      usedModel,
      fallbackOccurred,

      refinementMeta: {
        passes,

        burstinessBefore: diagBefore.burstiness,
        burstinessAfter: diagAfter.burstiness,

        clichesBefore: diagBefore.foundCliches.length,
        clichesAfter: diagAfter.foundCliches.length,

        detectorAiBefore,
        detectorAiAfter,

        overallScoreAfter: diagAfter.overallScore,

        factsPreservedPct: diagAfter.factsReport.preservedPct,
      },
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Transform error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà òðàíñôîðìàöèè òåêñòà');
  }
});

app.post('/api/generate-draft', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      text,
      strength = 10,
      customInstructions = '',
      shields = {},
      model = 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
    } = req.body;

    if (typeof text !== 'string' || !text.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Òåêñò îáÿçàòåëåí',
      });
    }

    const normalizedModel = normalizeModelId(model);
    const draftArchetype = getGenreArchetypeProfile('draft');

    const systemInstruction = `Òû  ìàñòåð íàó÷íîé ñòèëèçàöèè è ñîçäàíèÿ àóòåíòè÷íûõ èññëåäîâàòåëüñêèõ ÷åðíîâèêîâ.
Òâîÿ öåëü  ïåðåïèñàòü èñõîäíûé òåêñò â ôîðìå æèâîãî àêàäåìè÷åñêîãî ÷åðíîâèêà óðîâíÿ ${strength} (èç 10).
${customInstructions ? `Óêàçàíèÿ: ${customInstructions}` : ''}

Îñîáåííîñòè óðîâíÿ ÷åðíîâèêà ${strength}:
- Æèâîé õîä ìûñëè àâòîðà, ïîèñê òî÷íûõ ôîðìóëèðîâîê, åñòåñòâåííûå ñâÿçêè, âíóòðåííÿÿ äèíàìèêà èññëåäîâàíèÿ.
- Âûñîêàÿ ñèíòàêñè÷åñêàÿ âàðèàòèâíîñòü (Burstiness ~${draftArchetype.burstiness}): ÷åðåäóé êîðîòêèå âûâîäû (3-6 ñëîâ) è ðàçâåðíóòûå ïåðèîäû (24+ ñëîâ). Îðèåíòèð îòãëàãîëüíûõ ñóùåñòâèòåëüíûõ  íå âûøå ${draftArchetype.nominalizationPer100}/100 ñëîâ.
- Îòñóòñòâèå çàãëàæåííûõ ôîðìóëèðîâîê, òðèàä, öåïî÷åê ðîäèòåëüíûõ ïàäåæåé è ÈÈ-ñòåðåîòèïîâ («â ðàìêàõ», «ñëåäóåò îòìåòèòü», «ÿâëÿåòñÿ»). Îáû÷íûå ñâÿçêè («òàêèì îáðàçîì», «â ñâîþ î÷åðåäü») äîïóñòèìû, íî íå ÷àùå îäíîãî ðàçà íà àáçàö.
- Ïîëíîå ñîõðàíåíèå âñåõ íàó÷íûõ àðãóìåíòîâ, äàò, èìåí, îòðèöàíèé è òåçèñîâ.

${buildFewShotContrastBlock()}

Âûâåäè òîëüêî ãîòîâûé òåêñò ÷åðíîâèêà áåç ïðåàìáóë.`;

    const pass1 = await generateWithFallback(
      normalizedModel,
      text,
      systemInstruction,
      'high',
      { temperature: 1.02, topP: 0.95, frequencyPenalty: 0.25, presencePenalty: 0.15 }
    );

    let finalText = deterministicPolish(pass1.text);

    let usedModel = pass1.usedModel;
    let fallbackOccurred = pass1.fallbackOccurred;

    if (strength >= 7) {
      const initialDiagnostic = diagnoseTextDefects(
        finalText,
        undefined,
        text
      );

      const loopRes = await runGenericConvergenceLoop({
        initialText: finalText,
        sourceText: text,
        model: usedModel,
        maxCycles: 3,
        initialDiagnostic,
        shields,
      });

      finalText = loopRes.finalText;

      usedModel = loopRes.usedModel;

      fallbackOccurred =
        fallbackOccurred || loopRes.fallbackOccurred;
    }

    res.json({
      draft: finalText,
      usedModel,
      fallbackOccurred,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Draft error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà ãåíåðàöèè ÷åðíîâèêà');
  }
});

app.post('/api/academic-edit', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      text,
      customInstructions = '',
      shields = {},
      model = 'gemini-3.1-flash-lite',
    } = req.body;

    if (typeof text !== 'string' || !text.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Òåêñò îáÿçàòåëåí',
      });
    }

    const normalizedModel = normalizeModelId(model);
    const academicArchetype = getGenreArchetypeProfile('academic');

    const systemInstruction = `Òû  ñòðîãèé àêàäåìè÷åñêèé ðåäàêòîð, ïðîâåðÿþùèé òåêñò ïî 39 ïðàâèëàì íàó÷íîãî ðåãëàìåíòà:
1. Çàïðåò íà øòàìïîâàííûå ïåðåõîäíûå ìîñòèêè («ñëåäóåò îòìåòèòü», «â ðàìêàõ», «äàííûé êîíòåêñò»). Îáû÷íûå ñâÿçêè («òàêèì îáðàçîì», «â ñâîþ î÷åðåäü») äîïóñòèìû, íî íå ÷àùå îäíîãî ðàçà íà àáçàö.
2. Âûñîêèé ïîêàçàòåëü ïðåðûâèñòîñòè (burstiness ~${academicArchetype.burstiness}): ñî÷åòàíèå ðóáëåíûõ àêöåíòîâ (3-6 ñëîâ) è ñëîæíûõ ïåðèîäîâ (24+ ñëîâ). Äåðæè äîëþ îòãëàãîëüíûõ ñóùåñòâèòåëüíûõ óìåðåííîé (~${academicArchetype.nominalizationPer100}/100 ñë.).
3. Çàïðåò íà ãëàäêèå, ñèììåòðè÷íûå è îáîáùàþùèå ôèíàëû àáçàöåâ.
4. Êîíêðåòèçàöèÿ íàó÷íûõ ïîëîæåíèé, òî÷íîñòü òåðìèíîëîãèè, îòñóòñòâèå ïóñòîé âîäû è öåïî÷åê ðîäèòåëüíûõ ïàäåæåé.
5. Çàìåíà èñêóññòâåííîãî ãëàãîëà «ÿâëÿåòñÿ» íà òèðå «» èëè òî÷íûå ïðåäèêàòû.
6. Æèâàÿ àâòîðñêàÿ èíòîíàöèÿ áåç ïàññèâíîãî êàíöåëÿðèòà è íàäóìàííûõ òðèàä.
${customInstructions ? `Ñïåöèàëüíûå óêàçàíèÿ: ${customInstructions}` : ''}

${buildFewShotContrastBlock()}

Âûâåäè îòðåäàêòèðîâàííûé òåêñò áåç êîììåíòàðèåâ.`;

    const pass1 = await generateWithFallback(
      normalizedModel,
      text,
      systemInstruction,
      'high',
      { temperature: 0.88, topP: 0.94, frequencyPenalty: 0.22, presencePenalty: 0.1 }
    );

    const initialText = deterministicPolish(pass1.text);

    const initialDiagnostic = diagnoseTextDefects(
      initialText,
      undefined,
      text
    );

    const loopRes = await runGenericConvergenceLoop({
      initialText,
      sourceText: text,
      model: pass1.usedModel,
      maxCycles: 4,
      initialDiagnostic,
      shields,
    });

    res.json({
      editedText: loopRes.finalText,
      usedModel: loopRes.usedModel,
      fallbackOccurred:
        pass1.fallbackOccurred || loopRes.fallbackOccurred,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('Academic edit error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà àêàäåìè÷åñêîãî ðåäàêòèðîâàíèÿ');
  }
});

/* 5. SLOPTOTAL FULL INTEGRATION + AUDIT ÄÅÒÅÊÒÎÐÎÂ                    */

const MAX_SLOPTOTAL_REPORTS = 100;
const slopReportsStore = new Map<
  string,
  { report: SlopAnalysisReport; feedback?: { verdict: 'human' | 'ai' | 'mixed' | 'unsure'; note?: string; submittedAt: string } }
>();
const slopAggregateStats = {
  totalAnalyses: 0,
  totalQuickScans: 0,
  totalParagraphScans: 0,
  totalSiteScans: 0,
  verdictCounts: { clean: 0, low: 0, suspicious: 0, likely: 0, slop: 0 } as Record<string, number>,
  feedbackCounts: { human: 0, ai: 0, mixed: 0, unsure: 0 } as Record<string, number>,
};

function isPrivateOrBlockedIp(ip: string): boolean {
  const cleanIp = ip.toLowerCase();
  if (cleanIp === '::1' || cleanIp === '::' || cleanIp.startsWith('fe80:') || cleanIp.startsWith('fc') || cleanIp.startsWith('fd')) {
    return true;
  }
  const v4 = cleanIp.startsWith('::ffff:') ? cleanIp.slice(7) : cleanIp;
  if (net.isIPv4(v4)) {
    const parts = v4.split('.').map(Number);
    const [a, b] = parts;
    if (a === 0 || a === 10 || a === 127) return true;
    if (a === 169 && b === 254) return true;
    if (a === 172 && b >= 16 && b <= 31) return true;
    if (a === 192 && b === 168) return true;
    if (a === 100 && b >= 64 && b <= 127) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

async function safeFetchPublicUrl(rawUrl: string): Promise<{
  finalUrl: string;
  html: string;
  headers: Record<string, string>;
  status: number;
}> {
  let currentUrl: URL;
  try {
    currentUrl = new URL(rawUrl.trim());
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Íåêîððåêòíûé URL. Óêàæèòå àäðåñ ñ http:// èëè https://');
  }

  if (currentUrl.protocol !== 'http:' && currentUrl.protocol !== 'https:') {
    throw new Error('Ðàçðåøåíû òîëüêî ïðîòîêîëû http:// è https://');
  }

  const MAX_REDIRECTS = 3;
  const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

  for (let hop = 0; hop <= MAX_REDIRECTS; hop++) {
    const hostname = currentUrl.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (
      hostname === 'localhost' ||
      hostname === 'metadata.google.internal' ||
      hostname.endsWith('.local') ||
      hostname.endsWith('.internal')
    ) {
      throw new Error('Äîñòóï ê ëîêàëüíûì èëè âíóòðåííèì àäðåñàì çàïðåù¸í');
    }

    if (net.isIP(hostname)) {
      if (isPrivateOrBlockedIp(hostname)) {
        throw new Error('Çàáëîêèðîâàí ïðèâàòíûé èëè loopback IP-àäðåñ');
      }
    } else {
      const resolved = await dns.promises.lookup(hostname, { all: true });
      if (!resolved || resolved.length === 0) {
        throw new Error(`Íå óäàëîñü ðàçðåøèòü äîìåí: ${hostname}`);
      }
      for (const entry of resolved) {
        if (isPrivateOrBlockedIp(entry.address)) {
          throw new Error(`Äîìåí ${hostname} óêàçûâàåò íà ïðèâàòíûé IP (${entry.address})`);
        }
      }
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15_000);
    let response: Response;
    let buf: Buffer | null = null;

    try {
      response = await fetch(currentUrl.toString(), {
        method: 'GET',
        redirect: 'manual',
        signal: controller.signal,
        headers: {
          'User-Agent': 'SlopTotalBot/1.0 (+https://github.com/pablocaeg/sloptotal)',
          Accept: 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,text/plain;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
        },
      });

      if (response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400) {
        const loc = response.headers.get('location');
        if (!loc) {
          throw new Error(`Ñåðâåð âåðíóë ðåäèðåêò ${response.status} áåç çàãîëîâêà Location`);
        }
        currentUrl = new URL(loc, currentUrl);
        if (currentUrl.protocol !== 'http:' && currentUrl.protocol !== 'https:') {
          throw new Error('Ðåäèðåêò âåä¸ò íà çàïðåù¸ííûé ïðîòîêîë');
        }
        await response.body?.cancel();
        continue;
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Ñòðàíèöà âåðíóëà HTTP ${response.status}`);
      }

      const contentLength = Number(response.headers.get('content-length') || 0);
      if (contentLength > MAX_BYTES) {
        throw new Error('Ðàçìåð ñòðàíèöû ïðåâûøàåò ëèìèò 5 ÌÁ');
      }

      // Îãðàíè÷èâàåì èìåííî ïîòîê ÷òåíèÿ, à íå ïðîâåðÿåì ðàçìåð ïîñëå çàãðóçêè
      // âñåãî îòâåòà â ïàìÿòü. Òàéìåð äåéñòâóåò è âî âðåìÿ ïîëó÷åíèÿ òåëà.
      const reader = response.body?.getReader();
      if (!reader) {
        buf = Buffer.alloc(0);
      } else {
        const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
        let totalBytes = 0;

        try {
          while (true) {
            const { done, value } = await reader.read();
            if (done) break;
            if (!value) continue;

            totalBytes += value.byteLength;
            if (totalBytes > MAX_BYTES) {
              await reader.cancel();
              throw new Error('Ðàçìåð îòâåòà ïðåâûøàåò ëèìèò 5 ÌÁ');
            }

            chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
          }
        } finally {
          reader.releaseLock();
        }

        buf = Buffer.concat(chunks, totalBytes);
      }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    if (!buf) {
      throw new Error('Íå óäàëîñü ïðî÷èòàòü òåëî îòâåòà');
    }

    const headersObj: Record<string, string> = {};
    response.headers.forEach((v, k) => {
      headersObj[k.toLowerCase()] = v;
    });

    return {
      finalUrl: currentUrl.toString(),
      html: buf.toString('utf8'),
      headers: headersObj,
      status: response.status,
    };
  }

  throw new Error('Ñëèøêîì ìíîãî ðåäèðåêòîâ ïðè çàãðóçêå URL');
}






      sourceLabel = fetched.finalUrl;

      if (!textToAnalyze || textToAnalyze.length < SLOPTOTAL_BANDS.MIN_TEXT_LENGTH) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          error:
            'Íà ñòðàíèöå íåäîñòàòî÷íî ÷èòàåìîãî òåêñòà (ìåíåå 50 ñèìâîëîâ). Âîçìîæíî, ñàéò ðåíäåðèòñÿ òîëüêî ÷åðåç JS  ñêîïèðóéòå òåêñò èëè HTML âðó÷íóþ.',
        });
      }
    }

    if (!textToAnalyze || textToAnalyze.length < SLOPTOTAL_BANDS.MIN_TEXT_LENGTH) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: `Ââåäèòå ìèíèìóì ${SLOPTOTAL_BANDS.MIN_TEXT_LENGTH} ñèìâîëîâ äëÿ àíàëèçà SlopTotal.`,
      });
    }

    if (typeof remoteUrl === 'string' && remoteUrl.trim()) {
      const cleanRemote = remoteUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');

      if (cleanRemote.startsWith('http://') || cleanRemote.startsWith('https://')) {
        try {
          const controller = new AbortController();
          const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30_000);

          try {
            const remoteResp = await fetch(`${cleanRemote}/api/analyze`, {
              method: 'POST',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              signal: controller.signal,
              body: JSON.stringify({
                text: sourceType === 'text' ? textToAnalyze : undefined,
                url: sourceType === 'url' ? sourceLabel : undefined,
                fast: Boolean(fast),
                engines:
                  Array.isArray(selectedEngineKeys) &&
                  selectedEngineKeys.length > 0
                    ? selectedEngineKeys
                    : undefined,
              }),
            });

            if (remoteResp.ok) {
              const remoteData: any = await remoteResp.json();

              if (
                remoteData &&
                typeof remoteData.overall_score === 'number'
              ) {
                const report: SlopAnalysisReport = {
                  id: remoteData.id || `remote-${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
                  sourceType,
                  source: sourceLabel || textToAnalyze.slice(0, 100),
                  textExcerpt: remoteData.text_excerpt || textToAnalyze,
                  wordCount:
                    remoteData.word_count ||
                    textToAnalyze.split(/\s+/).length,
                  inputChars: textToAnalyze.length,
                  language: remoteData.language || 'en',
                  languageSupport:
                    remoteData.language_support || 'supported',
                  overallScore: Number(remoteData.overall_score),
                  humanScore: Number(
                    (100 - Number(remoteData.overall_score)).toFixed(1)
                  ),
                  overallVerdict:
                    remoteData.overall_verdict || 'Remote SlopTotal',
                  verdictKey:
                    remoteData.overall_score <= 30
                      ? 'clean'
                      : remoteData.overall_score <= 45
                        ? 'low'
                        : remoteData.overall_score <= 55
                          ? 'suspicious'
                          : remoteData.overall_score <= 80
                            ? 'likely'
                            : 'slop',
                  confidence: remoteData.confidence || 'high',
                  scoreRange:
                    remoteData.score_range || [
                      remoteData.overall_score,
                      remoteData.overall_score,
                    ],
                  enginesFlagged: remoteData.engines_flagged || 0,
                  enginesTotal:
                    remoteData.engines_total ||
                    (remoteData.engine_results?.length || 0),
                  selectedKeys: (remoteData.engine_results || []).map(
                    (r: any) => r.engine_name
                  ),
                  matchedMarkers: [],
                  engineResults: (remoteData.engine_results || []).map(
                    (r: any) => {
                      const meta = SLOPTOTAL_ENGINES.find(
                        (m) =>
                          m.name === r.engine_name ||
                          m.key === r.engine_name
                      );

                      return {
                        key: meta?.key || r.engine_name,
                        engineName: r.engine_name,
                        code: meta?.code || r.engine_name,
                        engineType:
                          r.engine_type ||
                          meta?.engineType ||
                          'classifier',
                        score: Number(r.score),
                        verdict: r.verdict || 'clean',
                        details: r.details || '',
                        description: meta?.description || '',
                        url: meta?.url,
                        elapsedMs: 0,
                      };
                    }
                  ),
                  notes: [
                    ...(remoteData.notes || []),
                    `Âûïîëíåíî ÷åðåç âíåøíèé ñåðâåð SlopTotal (${cleanRemote})`,
                  ],
                  createdAt:
                    remoteData.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
                  elapsedMs: 0,
                  mode: 'remote',
                };

                storeSlopReport(report);
                return res.json(report);
              }
            }
          } finally {
            clearTimeout(timer);
          }
        } catch (remoteErr: any) {
          console.warn(
            'Remote SlopTotal fallback to embedded core:',
            remoteErr?.message
          );
        }
      }
    }

    const report = runSlopTotalAnalysis(textToAnalyze, {
      fast: Boolean(fast),
      selectedEngineKeys:
        Array.isArray(selectedEngineKeys) &&
        selectedEngineKeys.length > 0
          ? selectedEngineKeys
          : undefined,
    });

    report.sourceType = sourceType;
    report.source = sourceLabel || textToAnalyze.slice(0, 100);

    storeSlopReport(report);
    return res.json(report);
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('SlopTotal analyze error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà àíàëèçà SlopTotal');
  }
});

app.post('/api/sloptotal/site-scan', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { url = '' } = req.body || {};

    if (typeof url !== 'string' || !url.trim()) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Óêàæèòå URL ñàéòà.',
      });
    }

    const fetched = await safeFetchPublicUrl(url.trim());
    const extracted = extractHtmlFeaturesAndText(fetched.html);
    const pageType = classifyPageType(extracted);

    const report = {
      url: fetched.finalUrl,
      status: fetched.status,
      title: extracted.title,
      description: extracted.description,
      textLength: extracted.text.length,
      wordCount: extracted.text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length,
      pageType,
      builders: detectSiteBuilders(fetched.html, fetched.headers),
      headers: fetched.headers,
    };

    slopAggregateStats.totalSiteScans += 1;

    return res.json(report);
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('SlopTotal site scan error:', err);
    sendError(res, err, 'Îøèáêà àíàëèçà ñàéòà');
  }
});

app.get('/api/sloptotal/report/:id', (req, res) => {
  const item = slopReportsStore.get(req.params.id);

  if (!item) {
    return res.status(404).json({
      error: 'Îò÷¸ò íå íàéäåí èëè ñðîê åãî õðàíåíèÿ èñò¸ê.',
    });
  }

  return res.json(item);
});

app.post('/api/sloptotal/feedback/:id', (req, res) => {
  const item = slopReportsStore.get(req.params.id);

  if (!item) {
    return res.status(404).json({
      error: 'Îò÷¸ò íå íàéäåí.',
    });
  }

  const { verdict, note = '' } = req.body || {};
  const allowedVerdicts = ['human', 'ai', 'mixed', 'unsure'];

  if (!allowedVerdicts.includes(verdict)) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'Íåäîïóñòèìûé âàðèàíò îáðàòíîé ñâÿçè.',
    });
  }

  item.feedback = {
    verdict,
    note: typeof note === 'string' ? note.slice(0, 1000) : '',
    submittedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  slopAggregateStats.feedbackCounts[verdict] =
    (slopAggregateStats.feedbackCounts[verdict] || 0) + 1;

  return res.json({
    success: true,
    feedback: item.feedback,
  });
});

app.post('/api/mgt-eval/benchmark', (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      samples,
      detectorKey = 'binoculars',
      threshold = 0.5,
      fitPlattCalibration = true,
    } = req.body || {};

    const parsed = parseMgtBenchmarkSamples(samples);
    const dataset =
      parsed.length >= 2
        ? parsed
        : MGT_SAMPLE_BENCHMARK_DATASET;

    const thr =
      typeof threshold === 'number' && Number.isFinite(threshold)
        ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, threshold))
        : 0.5;

    const report = evaluateMgtBenchmark(
      dataset,
      String(detectorKey || 'binoculars'),
      {
        threshold: thr,
        fitPlattCalibration: Boolean(fitPlattCalibration),
      }
    );

    if (report.plattParams) {
      savedPlattCalibrator = report.plattParams;
    }

    mgtEvalStats.totalBenchmarks += 1;

    res.json({
      ...report,
      usedSampleCount: dataset.length,
      usedDefaultDataset: parsed.length < 2,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('MGT-Eval benchmark error:', err);
    res.status(400).json({
      error: err?.message || 'Îøèáêà áåí÷ìàðêà MGT-Eval',
    });
  }
});

app.post('/api/mgt-eval/calibrate', (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      samples,
      detectorKey = 'binoculars',
    } = req.body || {};

    const parsed = parseMgtBenchmarkSamples(samples);

    const dataset =
      parsed.length >= 4
        ? parsed
        : MGT_SAMPLE_BENCHMARK_DATASET;

    const scores: number[] = [];
    const labels: number[] = [];

    for (const sample of dataset) {
      const detection = runMgtEvalDetection(sample.text, {
        detectorKeys: [String(detectorKey || 'binoculars')],
      });

      scores.push(
        detection.results[0]?.aiProbability ??
        detection.overallAiProbability
      );

      labels.push(sample.label > 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    }

    const params = fitPlatt(scores, labels);

    savedPlattCalibrator = params;
    mgtEvalStats.totalCalibrations += 1;

    res.json({
      detectorKey: String(detectorKey || 'binoculars'),
      params,
      sampleCount: dataset.length,
      usedDefaultDataset: parsed.length < 4,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('MGT-Eval calibrate error:', err);
    res.status(400).json({
      error: err?.message || 'Îøèáêà êàëèáðîâêè Platt Scaling',
    });
  }
});

app.post('/api/mgt-eval/attack', (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      text = '',
      attackType = 'homo',
      pct = 0.25,
      seed = 42,
      detectorKeys,
      threshold = 0.5,
    } = req.body || {};

    const cleanText =
      typeof text === 'string'
        ? text.trim()
        : '';

    if (cleanText.length < 15) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: 'Ââåäèòå ìèíèìóì 15 ñèìâîëîâ äëÿ ñèìóëÿöèè àòàêè è ðàñ÷¸òà ASR.',
      });
    }

    const validAttack = MGT_ATTACK_CATALOG.some(
      (attack) => attack.type === attackType
    )
      ? (attackType as MgtAttackType)
      : 'homo';

    const ratio =
      typeof pct === 'number' && Number.isFinite(pct)
        ? Math.max(0.05, Math.min(0.8, pct))
        : 0.25;

    const thr =
      typeof threshold === 'number' && Number.isFinite(threshold)
        ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, threshold))
        : 0.5;

    const validKeys = Array.isArray(detectorKeys)
      ? detectorKeys.filter(
          (key: unknown): key is string =>
            typeof key === 'string'
        )
      : undefined;

    const numericSeed = Number(seed);
    const safeSeed =
      Number.isFinite(numericSeed)
        ? numericSeed
        : 42;

    const attackedText = applySingleMgtAttack(
      cleanText,
      validAttack,
      ratio,
      safeSeed
    );

    const qualityBefore = evaluateMgtQuality(cleanText);
    const qualityAfter = evaluateMgtQuality(
      attackedText,
      cleanText
    );

    const beforeReport = runMgtEvalDetection(cleanText, {
      detectorKeys: validKeys,
      threshold: thr,
    });

    const afterReport = runMgtEvalDetection(attackedText, {
      detectorKeys: validKeys,
      threshold: thr,
    });

    let previouslyFlagged = 0;
    let evadedCount = 0;

    const comparison = beforeReport.results.map(
      (beforeResult, index) => {
        const afterResult = afterReport.results[index];

        const beforePrediction = beforeResult.prediction;
        const afterPrediction =
          afterResult?.prediction || beforePrediction;

        const flippedToHuman =
          beforePrediction === 'ai' &&
          afterPrediction === 'human';

        if (beforePrediction === 'ai') {
          previouslyFlagged += 1;
        }

        if (flippedToHuman) {
          evadedCount += 1;
        }

        const afterProbability =
          afterResult?.aiProbability ??
          beforeResult.aiProbability;

        return {
          key: beforeResult.key,
          name: beforeResult.name,
          type: beforeResult.type,

          beforeAiProb: beforeResult.aiProbability,
          afterAiProb: afterProbability,

          deltaAiProb: Number(
            (afterProbability - beforeResult.aiProbability).toFixed(4)
          ),

          beforePrediction,
          afterPrediction,
          flippedToHuman,
        };
      }
    );

    const asr =
      previouslyFlagged > 0
        ? Number(
            (evadedCount / previouslyFlagged).toFixed(4)
          )
        : 0;

    const avgAiDrop = Number(
      (
        beforeReport.overallAiProbability -
        afterReport.overallAiProbability
      ).toFixed(4)
    );

    mgtEvalStats.totalAttacks += 1;

    res.json({
      attackType: validAttack,
      pct: ratio,

      originalText: cleanText,
      attackedText,

      qualityBefore,
      qualityAfter,

      beforeOverallAi: beforeReport.overallAiProbability,
      afterOverallAi: afterReport.overallAiProbability,

      avgAiDrop,
      previouslyFlagged,
      evadedCount,
      asr,

      comparison,
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('MGT-Eval attack error:', err);
    res.status(400).json({
      error: err?.message || 'Îøèáêà ñèìóëÿöèè àòàêè MGT-Eval',
    });
  }
});

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* 5C. MGTBENCH FULL INTEGRATION                                       */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

const mgtBenchStats = {
  totalDetections: 0,
  totalAttributions: 0,
  totalBenchmarks: 0,
};

function parseMgtBenchSamples(
  rawInput: unknown
): MgtBenchSample[] {
  if (Array.isArray(rawInput)) {
    const out: MgtBenchSample[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < rawInput.length; i++) {
      const item: any = rawInput[i];

      if (
        item &&
        typeof item.text === 'string' &&
        item.text.trim()
      ) {
        const label = Number(item.label) > 0.5 ? 1 : 0;

        out.push({
          dataset:
            typeof item.dataset === 'string'
              ? item.dataset
              : 'Custom',

          llm:
            typeof item.llm === 'string'
              ? item.llm
              : label === 1
                ? 'ChatGPT'
                : 'Human',

          label,
          text: item.text.trim(),
        });
      }
    }

    return out;
  }

  if (
    typeof rawInput === 'string' &&
    rawInput.trim()
  ) {
    const lines = rawInput
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const out: MgtBenchSample[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const line = lines[i];

      if (line.startsWith('{')) {
        try {
          const obj = JSON.parse(line);

          if (
            obj &&
            typeof obj.text === 'string' &&
            obj.text.trim()
          ) {
            const label = Number(obj.label) > 0.5 ? 1 : 0;

            out.push({
              dataset:
                typeof obj.dataset === 'string'
                  ? obj.dataset
                  : 'Custom',

              llm:
                typeof obj.llm === 'string'
                  ? obj.llm
                  : label === 1
                    ? 'ChatGPT'
                    : 'Human',

              label,
              text: obj.text.trim(),
            });
          }

          continue;
        } catch {
          // Ïðè îøèáêå JSON ïðîáóåì ðàçîáðàòü ñòðîêó êàê CSV/TSV.
        }
      }

      if (
        i === 0 &&
        /^label[,;\t]/i.test(line)
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      const tabParts = line.split('\t');

      if (
        tabParts.length >= 2 &&
        /^[01]$/.test(tabParts[0].trim())
      ) {
        const label = Number(tabParts[0].trim());

        out.push({
          dataset: 'TSV',
          llm: label === 1 ? 'ChatGPT' : 'Human',
          label,
          text: tabParts.slice(1).join('\t').trim(),
        });

        continue;
      }

      const csvMatch = line.match(
        /^([01])\s*[,;]\s*"?(.*?)"?$/
      );

      if (csvMatch && csvMatch[2].trim()) {
        const label = Number(csvMatch[1]);

        out.push({
          dataset: 'CSV',
          llm: label === 1 ? 'ChatGPT' : 'Human',
          label,
          text: csvMatch[2].replace(/""/g, '"').trim(),
        });
      }
    }

    return out;
  }

  return [];
}

    datasets: MGTBENCH_DATASETS,

    sampleCorpus: MGTBENCH_BUILTIN_CORPUS,

  });
